		private void btnOK_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			TreeNode selectedNode = treeView1.SelectedNode;
			string text = selectedNode.Text;
			if (selectedNode.Parent != null)
			{
				while (selectedNode.Parent.Parent != null)
				{
					selectedNode = selectedNode.Parent;
					text = string.Concat(selectedNode.Text + ".", text);
					if (selectedNode.Parent == null)
					{
						break;
					}
				}
			}
			SelectedItem = text;
			Close();
		}

		public unsafe void SetHierarchies(CArray<ATL::CStringT<wchar_t,StrTraitMFC_DLL<wchar_t,ATL::ChTraitsCRT<wchar_t> > >,ATL::CStringT<wchar_t,StrTraitMFC_DLL<wchar_t,ATL::ChTraitsCRT<wchar_t> > > const &>* arrStrHierarchies)

		private void InsertNodeToTreeView(TreeNode parentNode, string strText)
		{
			if (parentNode == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (!strText.Contains("."))
			{
				int num = 0;
				if (0 < parentNode.Nodes.Count)
				{
					do
					{
						if (!(parentNode.Nodes[num].Text == strText))
						{
							num++;
							continue;
						}
						GC.KeepAlive(this);
						return;
					}
					while (num < parentNode.Nodes.Count);
				}
				TreeNode node = new TreeNode(strText);
				parentNode.Nodes.Add(node);
				GC.KeepAlive(this);
			}
			else if (parentNode.Nodes.Count == 0)
			{
				if (strText.Contains("."))
				{
					do
					{
						TreeNode treeNode = new TreeNode(strText.Substring(0, strText.IndexOf('.')));
						parentNode.Nodes.Add(treeNode);
						parentNode = treeNode;
						string obj = strText;
						strText = obj.Substring(obj.IndexOf('.') + 1);
					}
					while (strText.Contains("."));
				}
				parentNode.Nodes.Add(new TreeNode(strText));
			}
			else
			{
				TreeNode parentNode2 = FindParentNode(parentNode, ref strText);
				InsertNodeToTreeView(parentNode2, strText);
			}
		}

		private TreeNode FindParentNode(TreeNode node, ref string strText)
		{
			if (strText.Contains(".") && node.Nodes.Count != 0)
			{
				string text = strText.Substring(0, strText.IndexOf('.'));
				int num = 0;
				if (0 < node.Nodes.Count)
				{
					do
					{
						if (!(node.Nodes[num].Text == text))
						{
							num++;
							continue;
						}
						strText = strText.Substring(strText.IndexOf('.') + 1);
						TreeNode result = FindParentNode(node.Nodes[num], ref strText);
						GC.KeepAlive(this);
						return result;
					}
					while (num < node.Nodes.Count);
				}
				GC.KeepAlive(this);
				return null;
			}
			GC.KeepAlive(this);
			return node;
		}

		[HandleProcessCorruptedStateExceptions]

					base.Dispose(disposing: true);
				}
			}
			base.Dispose(disposing: false);
		}

			: base(message)
		{
		}
	}
	[Serializable]
	internal class ModuleLoadExceptionHandlerException : <CrtImplementationDetails>.ModuleLoadException
	{
		private const string formatString = "\n{0}: {1}\n--- Start of primary exception ---\n{2}\n--- End of primary exception ---\n\n--- Start of nested exception ---\n{3}\n--- End of nested exception ---\n";

		private Exception <backing_store>NestedException;

		public Exception NestedException
		{
			get
			{
				return <backing_store>NestedException;
			}
			set
			{
				<backing_store>NestedException = value;
			}
		}

		protected ModuleLoadExceptionHandlerException(SerializationInfo info, StreamingContext context)
			: base(info, context)
		{
			Type typeFromHandle = typeof(Exception);
			string name = "NestedException";
			NestedException = (Exception)info.GetValue(name, typeFromHandle);
			GC.KeepAlive(this);
		}

		public ModuleLoadExceptionHandlerException(string message, Exception innerException, Exception nestedException)
			: base(message, innerException)
		{
			NestedException = nestedException;
		}

		public override string ToString()
		{
			string text = ((InnerException == null) ? string.Empty : InnerException.ToString());
			string text2 = ((NestedException == null) ? string.Empty : NestedException.ToString());

			Type type = GetType();
			array[0] = type;
			string text3 = ((Message == null) ? string.Empty : Message);
			array[1] = text3;

			array[2] = text4;
			string text5 = ((text2 == null) ? string.Empty : text2);
			array[3] = text5;
			string result = string.Format("\n{0}: {1}\n--- Start of primary exception ---\n{2}\n--- End of primary exception ---\n\n--- Start of nested exception ---\n{3}\n--- End of nested exception ---\n", array);

		}

	internal class ModuleUninitializer : Stack
	{
		private static object @lock;

		internal static <CrtImplementationDetails>.ModuleUninitializer _ModuleUninitializer;

		[SecuritySafeCritical]
		internal void AddHandler(EventHandler handler)
		{
			bool lockTaken = false;
			RuntimeHelpers.PrepareConstrainedRegions();
			try
			{
				RuntimeHelpers.PrepareConstrainedRegions();
				Monitor.Enter(@lock, ref lockTaken);
				RuntimeHelpers.PrepareDelegate(handler);
				Push(handler);
			}
			finally
			{
				if (lockTaken)
				{
					Monitor.Exit(@lock);
				}
			}
		}

		[SecurityCritical]
		static ModuleUninitializer()
		{
			@lock = new object();
			_ModuleUninitializer = new <CrtImplementationDetails>.ModuleUninitializer();
		}

		[SecuritySafeCritical]
		private ModuleUninitializer()
		{
			EventHandler value = SingletonDomainUnload;
			AppDomain.CurrentDomain.DomainUnload += value;
			AppDomain.CurrentDomain.ProcessExit += value;
		}

		[PrePrepareMethod]
		[SecurityCritical]
		private void SingletonDomainUnload(object source, EventArgs arguments)
		{
			bool lockTaken = false;
			RuntimeHelpers.PrepareConstrainedRegions();
			try
			{
				RuntimeHelpers.PrepareConstrainedRegions();
				Monitor.Enter(@lock, ref lockTaken);
				IEnumerator enumerator = GetEnumerator();
				try
				{
					while (enumerator.MoveNext())
					{
						((EventHandler)enumerator.Current)(source, arguments);
					}
				}
				finally
				{
					IEnumerator enumerator2 = enumerator;
					if (enumerator is IDisposable disposable)
					{
						disposable.Dispose();
					}
				}
			}
			finally
			{
				if (lockTaken)
				{
					Monitor.Exit(@lock);
				}
			}
		}
	}
